enum VpnErrorKind {
  config,
  binaryNotFound,
  permissions,
  processStart,
  processRuntime,
  disconnectedUnexpectedly,
  platformUnsupported,
  splitTunneling,
  unknown,
}

class VpnError implements Exception {
  VpnError(this.kind, this.message, {this.details});

  final VpnErrorKind kind;
  final String message;
  final Object? details;

  @override
  String toString() =>
      'VpnError(kind: $kind, message: $message, details: $details)';
}

// lib/core/logging/session_logger.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

class LogEntry {
  LogEntry({
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.level,
    required this.message,
  });

  final DateTime timestamp;
  final String level;
  final String message;

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => <String, dynamic>{
    'timestamp': timestamp.toIso8601String(),
    'level': level,
    'message': message,
  };

  @override
  String toString() => '[${timestamp.toIso8601String()}] [$level] $message';
}

class SessionLogger {
  SessionLogger({this.maxEntries = 500});

  final int maxEntries;
  final List<LogEntry> _entries = <LogEntry>[];

  List<LogEntry> get entries => List<LogEntry>.unmodifiable(_entries);

  String get allLogsAsText => _entries.map((e) => e.toString()).join('\n');

  void info(String message) => _add('INFO', message);
  void debug(String message) => _add('DEBUG', message);
  void warning(String message) => _add('WARN', message);
  void error(String message) => _add('ERROR', message);

  String exportAsJson() =>
      jsonEncode(_entries.map((entry) => entry.toJson()).toList());

  void clear() => _entries.clear();

  void _add(String level, String message) {
    final entry = LogEntry(
      timestamp: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
      level: level,
      message: message,
    );
    _entries.add(entry);

    // Print to stderr for debugging (visible in IDE console)
    stderr.writeln(entry.toString());

    // Trim if exceeds max
    while (_entries.length > maxEntries) {
      _entries.removeAt(0);
    }
  }
}

/// Server state for managing server list and selection
sealed class ServerState {
  const ServerState();
}

class ServerInitial extends ServerState {
  const ServerInitial();
}

  const ServerLoading();
}

class ServerLoaded extends ServerState {
  final List<ServerProfile> servers;
  final ServerProfile? selectedServer;
  final bool isRefreshing;

  const ServerLoaded({
    required this.servers,
    this.selectedServer,
    this.isRefreshing = false,
  });

  ServerLoaded copyWith({
    List<ServerProfile>? servers,
    ServerProfile? selectedServer,
    bool? isRefreshing,
  }) {
    return ServerLoaded(
      servers: servers ?? this.servers,
      selectedServer: selectedServer ?? this.selectedServer,
      isRefreshing: isRefreshing ?? this.isRefreshing,
    );
  }
}

/// Split tunnel state for managing split tunneling rules, excluded domains, and domain overrides
sealed class SplitTunnelState {
  const SplitTunnelState();
}

class SplitTunnelInitial extends SplitTunnelState {
  const SplitTunnelInitial();
}

class SplitTunnelLoaded extends SplitTunnelState {
  final SplitTunnelMode mode;
  final Set<String> selectedApps;
  final List<String> excludedDomains;
  final List<DomainOverride> domainOverrides;
  final bool isLoadingApps;

  const SplitTunnelLoaded({
    required this.mode,
    required this.selectedApps,
    required this.excludedDomains,
    required this.domainOverrides,
    this.isLoadingApps = false,
  });

  bool get isEnabled => mode != SplitTunnelMode.disabled;
  int get selectedAppCount => selectedApps.length;
  int get domainOverrideCount => domainOverrides.length;

  SplitTunnelLoaded copyWith({
    SplitTunnelMode? mode,
    Set<String>? selectedApps,
    List<String>? excludedDomains,
    List<DomainOverride>? domainOverrides,
    bool? isLoadingApps,
  }) {
    return SplitTunnelLoaded(
      mode: mode ?? this.mode,
      selectedApps: selectedApps ?? this.selectedApps,
      excludedDomains: excludedDomains ?? this.excludedDomains,
      domainOverrides: domainOverrides ?? this.domainOverrides,
      isLoadingApps: isLoadingApps ?? this.isLoadingApps,
    );
  }
}

class SplitTunnelError extends SplitTunnelState {
  final String message;
  const SplitTunnelError({required this.message});
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../logging/session_logger.dart';
import '../../vpn/vpn_interface.dart';
import '../../vpn/vpn_status.dart' as status;
import '../../vpn/models/server_models.dart';
import '../../vpn/config/config_generator.dart';
import '../../vpn/services/singbox_binary_service.dart';
import '../../vpn/windows_vpn.dart';
import '../errors/vpn_error.dart';

import 'vpn_state.dart';

/// VpnCubit manages VPN connection state using ChangeNotifier pattern
/// (simplified implementation without flutter_bloc dependency)
class VpnCubit extends ChangeNotifier {
  VpnCubit({required SessionLogger logger})
    : _logger = logger,
      _vpn = _buildVpn(logger);

  final SessionLogger _logger;
  final VpnInterface _vpn;
  StreamSubscription<status.VpnStatus>? _vpnSubscription;

  VpnCubitState _state = const VpnInitial();
  VpnCubitState get state => _state;

  String? _lastErrorMessage;
  String? get lastErrorMessage => _lastErrorMessage;

  void _setState(VpnCubitState newState) {
    _state = newState;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  static VpnInterface _buildVpn(SessionLogger logger) {
    if (Platform.isWindows) {
      return WindowsVpn(
        configGenerator: SingBoxConfigGenerator(),
        binaryService: SingBoxBinaryService(),
        logger: logger,
      );
    }
    throw VpnError(
      VpnErrorKind.platformUnsupported,
      'SpeedVPS supports Windows only',
    );
  }

  void initialize() {
    _vpnSubscription = _vpn.statusStream().listen((status) {
      _setState(status.toCubitState());
    });
  }

  Future<void> connect({
    required ServerProfile server,
    required SplitTunnelSettings splitTunnel,
    ServerProfile? albaniaProxy,
  }) async {
    _lastErrorMessage = null;
    try {
      _setState(const VpnConnecting());
      await _vpn.connect(
        server: server,
        splitTunnel: splitTunnel,
        albaniaProxy: albaniaProxy,
      );
    } on VpnError catch (error) {
      _lastErrorMessage = error.message;
      _logger.error(error.toString());
      _setState(VpnErrorState(message: error.message));
    } catch (error) {
      _lastErrorMessage = error.toString();
      _setState(VpnErrorState(message: _lastErrorMessage!));
    }
  }

  Future<void> disconnect() async {
    _setState(const VpnDisconnecting());
    await _vpn.disconnect();
    _setState(const VpnDisconnectedState());
  }

  status.VpnStatus get currentStatus => _vpn.getStatus();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _vpnSubscription?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

import '../../vpn/vpn_status.dart' as status;

/// States for VpnCubit - these represent the VPN connection states
/// Renamed from VpnState to avoid conflict with the enum in vpn_status.dart
sealed class VpnCubitState {
  const VpnCubitState();
}

class VpnInitial extends VpnCubitState {
  const VpnInitial();
}

class VpnConnecting extends VpnCubitState {
  const VpnConnecting();
}

class VpnConnectedState extends VpnCubitState {
  final DateTime connectedAt;
  const VpnConnectedState({required this.connectedAt});
}

class VpnDisconnecting extends VpnCubitState {
  const VpnDisconnecting();
}

class VpnDisconnectedState extends VpnCubitState {
  const VpnDisconnectedState();
}

class VpnReconnecting extends VpnCubitState {
  const VpnReconnecting();
}

class VpnErrorState extends VpnCubitState {
  final String message;
  const VpnErrorState({required this.message});
}

/// Extension to create VpnCubitState from VpnStatus
extension VpnStatusExtension on status.VpnStatus {
  VpnCubitState toCubitState() {
    switch (state) {
      case status.VpnState.disconnected:
        return const VpnDisconnectedState();
      case status.VpnState.connecting:
        return const VpnConnecting();
      case status.VpnState.connected:
        return VpnConnectedState(connectedAt: DateTime.now());
      case status.VpnState.disconnecting:
        return const VpnDisconnecting();
      case status.VpnState.error:
        return VpnErrorState(message: message ?? 'Unknown error');
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';

import '../vpn/models/server_models.dart';

class ServerConfig {
  final String name;
  final String address;
  final int port;
  final String uuid;
  final String serverName; // SNI
  final String publicKey;
  final String shortId;
  final String fingerprint;
  final String flow;

  const ServerConfig({
    required this.name,
    required this.address,
    required this.port,
    required this.uuid,
    required this.serverName,
    required this.publicKey,
    this.shortId = '',
    this.fingerprint = 'chrome',
    this.flow = 'xtls-rprx-vision',
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'name': name,
    'address': address,
    'port': port,
    'uuid': uuid,
    'serverName': serverName,
    'publicKey': publicKey,
    'shortId': shortId,
    'fingerprint': fingerprint,
    'flow': flow,
  };

  factory ServerConfig.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ServerConfig(
    name: json['name'] as String,
    address: json['address'] as String,
    port: json['port'] as int,
    uuid: json['uuid'] as String,
    serverName: json['serverName'] as String,
    publicKey: json['publicKey'] as String,
    shortId: json['shortId'] as String? ?? '',
    fingerprint: json['fingerprint'] as String? ?? 'chrome',
    flow: json['flow'] as String? ?? 'xtls-rprx-vision',
  );

  String encode() => jsonEncode(toJson());

  factory ServerConfig.decode(String source) =>
      ServerConfig.fromJson(jsonDecode(source) as Map<String, dynamic>);

  /// Parse a VLESS share link: vless://uuid@host:port?params#name
  factory ServerConfig.fromVlessUri(String uri) {
    final parsed = Uri.parse(uri);

    // 1. Проверка схемы
    if (parsed.scheme != 'vless') {
      throw FormatException(
        'Unsupported scheme: ${parsed.scheme}. Expected vless://',
      );
    }

    // 2. Извлечение обязательных компонентов
    final uuid = parsed.userInfo;
    if (uuid.isEmpty) {
      throw FormatException('UUID is missing in VLESS link');
    }

    final address = parsed.host;
    if (address.isEmpty) {
      throw FormatException('Server address is missing');
    }

    final port = parsed.hasPort ? parsed.port : 443;
    if (port <= 0 || port > 65535) {
      throw FormatException('Invalid port: $port');
    }

    // 3. Имя из фрагмента (может отсутствовать)
    final name = parsed.fragment.isNotEmpty
        ? Uri.decodeComponent(parsed.fragment)
        : address; // fallback на адрес

    // 4. Параметры запроса
    final params = parsed.queryParameters;

    return ServerConfig(
      name: name,
      address: address,
      port: port,
      uuid: uuid,
      serverName: params['sni'] ?? params['serverName'] ?? '',
      publicKey: params['pbk'] ?? '',
      shortId: params['sid'] ?? '',
      fingerprint: params['fp'] ?? 'chrome',
      flow: params['flow'] ?? '',
    );
  }

  String toVlessUri() {
    final params = <String, String>{
      'type': 'tcp',
      'security': 'reality',
      'sni': serverName,
      'pbk': publicKey,
      'fp': fingerprint,
      if (shortId.isNotEmpty) 'sid': shortId,
      if (flow.isNotEmpty) 'flow': flow,
    };
    final query = params.entries
        .map((e) => '${e.key}=${Uri.encodeComponent(e.value)}')
        .join('&');
    return 'vless://$uuid@$address:$port?$query#${Uri.encodeComponent(name)}';
  }

  /// Преобразует ServerConfig в ServerProfile для использования в VPN-движке
  ServerProfile toProfile() {
    return ServerProfile(
      id: '${address}_$port', // или можно использовать хеш от адреса/порта/uuid
      name: name,
      host: address,
      port: port,
      uuid: uuid,
      flow: flow,
      reality: RealitySettings(
        publicKey: publicKey,
        shortId: shortId,
        serverName: serverName,
        fingerprint: fingerprint,
      ),
    );
  }
}

  final double priceRub;

  const SubscriptionPlan({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.days,
    required this.priceRub,
    this.description,
  });

/// Информация о подписке пользователя
class SubscriptionInfo {
  DateTime? expireDate;
  String? vpnLink;
  String? marzbanUsername;
  bool autoRenew;
  String plan;
  DateTime createdAt;
  DateTime updatedAt;

  SubscriptionInfo({
    this.expireDate,
    this.vpnLink,
    this.marzbanUsername,
    this.autoRenew = true,
    this.plan = 'none',
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) : createdAt = createdAt ?? DateTime.now(),
       updatedAt = updatedAt ?? DateTime.now();

  bool get isActive =>
      expireDate != null && DateTime.now().isBefore(expireDate!);

  int get daysRemaining {
    if (expireDate == null) return 0;
    final diff = expireDate!.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
    return diff < 0 ? 0 : diff;
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'expire_date': expireDate?.toIso8601String(),
    'vpn_link': vpnLink,
    'marzban_username': marzbanUsername,
    'auto_renew': autoRenew,
    'plan': plan,
    'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
    'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
  };

  factory SubscriptionInfo.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      SubscriptionInfo(
        expireDate: json['expire_date'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(json['expire_date'])
            : null,
        vpnLink: json['vpn_link'],
        marzbanUsername: json['marzban_username'],
        autoRenew: json['auto_renew'] ?? true,
        plan: json['plan'] ?? 'none',
        createdAt: json['created_at'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(json['created_at'])
            : DateTime.now(),
        updatedAt: json['updated_at'] != null
            ? DateTime.parse(json['updated_at'])
            : DateTime.now(),
      );

  String toRawJson() => jsonEncode(toJson());
}

/// Ожидающий платёж
class PendingPayment {
  String paymentId;
  double amount;
  int days;
  String status;
  DateTime createdAt;
  String confirmationUrl;

  PendingPayment({
    required this.paymentId,
    required this.amount,
    required this.days,
    this.status = 'pending',
    required this.confirmationUrl,
    DateTime? createdAt,
  }) : createdAt = createdAt ?? DateTime.now();

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'payment_id': paymentId,
    'amount': amount,
    'days': days,
    'status': status,
    'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
    'confirmation_url': confirmationUrl,
  };

  factory PendingPayment.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => PendingPayment(
    paymentId: json['payment_id'],
    amount: (json['amount'] as num).toDouble(),
    days: json['days'],
    status: json['status'] ?? 'pending',
    confirmationUrl: json['confirmation_url'] ?? '',
    createdAt: json['created_at'] != null
        ? DateTime.parse(json['created_at'])
        : DateTime.now(),
  );
}

    required this.confirmationUrl,
    this.status = PaymentStatus.pending,
    required this.amount,

// lib/services/app_list_service.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class InstalledApp {
  const InstalledApp({
    required this.id,
    required this.displayName,
    required this.processName,
  });

  final String id;
  final String displayName;
  final String processName;

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'id': id,
    'displayName': displayName,
    'processName': processName,
  };

  factory InstalledApp.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => InstalledApp(
    id: json['id'] as String,
    displayName: json['displayName'] as String,
    processName: json['processName'] as String,
  );
}

class AppListService {
  static const String _selectedAppsKey = 'selected_apps';
  static const String _customAppsKey = 'custom_apps';
  static const String _cachedSystemAppsKey = 'cached_system_apps';

  List<InstalledApp>? _cache;

  /// Возвращает полный список приложений (системные + кастомные).
  Future<List<InstalledApp>> getInstalledApps({
    bool forceRefresh = false,
  }) async {
    if (!forceRefresh && _cache != null) {
      return _cache!;
    }

    final customApps = await _loadCustomApps();

    if (Platform.isWindows) {
      // Пытаемся загрузить кэшированный список системных приложений,
      // если не требуется принудительное обновление.
      List<InstalledApp> systemApps;
      if (!forceRefresh) {
        systemApps = await _loadCachedSystemApps();
      } else {
        systemApps = [];
      }

      // Если кэша нет или был запрошен forceRefresh, выполняем полное сканирование.
      if (systemApps.isEmpty) {
        systemApps = await _getWindowsApps();
        await _saveCachedSystemApps(systemApps);
      }

      // Объединяем системные и кастомные приложения.
      final allApps = <InstalledApp>[];
      final seenIds = <String>{};
      for (final app in [...customApps, ...systemApps]) {
        if (!seenIds.contains(app.id)) {
          seenIds.add(app.id);
          allApps.add(app);
        }
      }

      allApps.sort((a, b) => a.displayName.compareTo(b.displayName));
      _cache = allApps;
      return _cache!;
    }

    _cache = <InstalledApp>[];
    return _cache!;
  }

  // ---------- Кэширование системных приложений ----------
  Future<List<InstalledApp>> _loadCachedSystemApps() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final String? jsonString = prefs.getString(_cachedSystemAppsKey);
    if (jsonString == null || jsonString.isEmpty) return [];
    try {
      final List<dynamic> list = jsonDecode(jsonString);
      return list
          .map((e) => InstalledApp.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveCachedSystemApps(List<InstalledApp> apps) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final jsonString = jsonEncode(apps.map((a) => a.toJson()).toList());
    await prefs.setString(_cachedSystemAppsKey, jsonString);
  }

  // ---------- Получение системных приложений Windows ----------
  Future<List<InstalledApp>> _getWindowsApps() async {
    final apps = <InstalledApp>[];

    // 1. Быстрый запрос через Get-StartApps
    final result = await Process.run('powershell', [
      '-NoProfile',
      '-Command',
      'Get-StartApps | Select-Object Name,AppID | ConvertTo-Json -Compress',
    ], runInShell: true);

    if (result.exitCode == 0) {
      final output = result.stdout.toString().trim();
      if (output.isNotEmpty) {
        // Исправление: убираем final и присваиваем внутри try/catch
        List<dynamic> jsonList = <dynamic>[];
        try {
          jsonList = jsonDecode(output) as List<dynamic>;
        } catch (_) {
          final single = jsonDecode(output);
          jsonList = [single];
        }

        for (var item in jsonList) {
          final name = item['Name'] as String? ?? '';
          final appId = item['AppID'] as String? ?? '';
          if (name.isEmpty || appId.isEmpty) continue;

          String? exePath;
          if (appId.length >= 2 && appId[1] == ':') {
            exePath = appId;
          } else {
            continue; // пропускаем UWP/неточные пути
          }

          if (File(exePath).existsSync()) {
            final processName = exePath.split('\\').last;
            apps.add(
              InstalledApp(
                id: exePath,
                displayName: name,
                processName: processName,
              ),
            );
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Добавляем базовые системные утилиты
    final systemApps = [
      {'name': 'Notepad', 'path': r'C:\Windows\System32\notepad.exe'},
      {'name': 'Calculator', 'path': r'C:\Windows\System32\calc.exe'},
      {'name': 'Command Prompt', 'path': r'C:\Windows\System32\cmd.exe'},
      {
        'name': 'PowerShell',
        'path': r'C:\Windows\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe',
      },
    ];
    for (final sa in systemApps) {
      if (File(sa['path']!).existsSync()) {
        apps.add(
          InstalledApp(
            id: sa['path']!,
            displayName: sa['name']!,
            processName: sa['path']!.split('\\').last,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    // Удаляем дубликаты по id
    final unique = <String, InstalledApp>{};
    for (final app in apps) {
      unique[app.id] = app;
    }
    return unique.values.toList();
  }

  // ---------- Управление кастомными приложениями ----------
  Future<void> addCustomApp(InstalledApp app) async {
    final current = await _loadCustomApps();
    if (current.any((a) => a.id == app.id)) return;
    current.add(app);
    await _saveCustomApps(current);
    _cache = null; // сбрасываем кэш
  }

  /// Удаляет кастомное приложение по id
  Future<void> removeCustomApp(String id) async {
    final current = await _loadCustomApps();
    current.removeWhere((a) => a.id == id);
    await _saveCustomApps(current);
    _cache = null;
  }

  Future<List<InstalledApp>> _loadCustomApps() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final String? jsonString = prefs.getString(_customAppsKey);
    if (jsonString == null || jsonString.isEmpty) return [];
    try {
      final List<dynamic> list = jsonDecode(jsonString);
      return list
          .map((e) => InstalledApp.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> _saveCustomApps(List<InstalledApp> apps) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final jsonString = jsonEncode(apps.map((a) => a.toJson()).toList());
    await prefs.setString(_customAppsKey, jsonString);
  }

  // ---------- Сохранение / загрузка выбранных приложений ----------
  Future<void> saveSelectedApps(Set<String> selectedAppIds) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setStringList(_selectedAppsKey, selectedAppIds.toList());
  }

  Future<Set<String>> loadSelectedApps() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final list = prefs.getStringList(_selectedAppsKey) ?? [];
    return list.toSet();
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

import '../models/server_config.dart';

class ConfigStorage {
  static const _fileName = 'servers.json';

  Future<File> _getFile() async {
    final dir = await getApplicationSupportDirectory();
    return File('${dir.path}/$_fileName');
  }

  Future<List<ServerConfig>> loadServers() async {
    try {
      final file = await _getFile();
      if (!await file.exists()) return [];
      final content = await file.readAsString();
      final list = jsonDecode(content) as List;
      return list
          .map((e) => ServerConfig.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveServers(List<ServerConfig> servers) async {
    final file = await _getFile();
    final json = jsonEncode(servers.map((s) => s.toJson()).toList());
    await file.writeAsString(json, flush: true);
  }

  Future<void> addServer(ServerConfig server) async {
    final servers = await loadServers();
    servers.add(server);
    await saveServers(servers);
  }

  Future<void> removeServer(int index) async {
    final servers = await loadServers();
    if (index >= 0 && index < servers.length) {
      servers.removeAt(index);
      await saveServers(servers);
    }
  }
}

// lib/services/encrypted_config_storage.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:pointycastle/export.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../models/server_config.dart';

/// Result type for operations that can fail
class EncryptedResult<T> {
  EncryptedResult._(this._value, this._error);
  factory EncryptedResult.success(T value) => EncryptedResult._(value, null);
  factory EncryptedResult.failure(String error) =>
      EncryptedResult._(null, error);

  final T? _value;
  final String? _error;

  bool get isSuccess => _error == null;
  bool get isFailure => _error != null;
  T get value => _value as T;
  String get error => _error!;
}

/// Secure storage wrapper using AES-256-GCM encryption
/// Uses SharedPreferences instead of flutter_secure_storage for cross-platform compatibility
class EncryptedConfigStorage {
  static const String _keyStorageKey = 'encryption_key';
  static const String _serversKey = 'servers_encrypted';
  static const String _hmacKey = 'data_hmac';
  static const String _legacyServersKey = 'servers';
  static const int _keyLength = 32; // 256 bits
  static const int _ivLength = 12; // 96 bits for GCM

  SharedPreferences? _prefs;
  Uint8List? _encryptionKey;

  EncryptedConfigStorage({SharedPreferences? prefs}) : _prefs = prefs;

  /// Initialize storage and load or generate encryption key
  Future<EncryptedResult<void>> initialize() async {
    try {
      _prefs ??= await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      _encryptionKey = await _loadOrCreateKey();
      if (_encryptionKey == null) {
        return EncryptedResult.failure('Failed to initialize encryption key');
      }
      return EncryptedResult.success(null);
    } catch (e) {
      return EncryptedResult.failure('Initialization failed: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Load existing key or create new one
  Future<Uint8List?> _loadOrCreateKey() async {
    final existingKey = _prefs!.getString(_keyStorageKey);

    if (existingKey != null) {
      return base64Decode(existingKey);
    }

    // Generate new key
    final key = _generateSecureRandom(_keyLength);
    await _prefs!.setString(_keyStorageKey, base64Encode(key));
    return key;
  }

  /// Generate cryptographically secure random bytes
  Uint8List _generateSecureRandom(int length) {
    final random = Random.secure();
    return Uint8List.fromList(
      List<int>.generate(length, (_) => random.nextInt(256)),
    );
  }

  /// Encrypt data using AES-256-GCM
  String _encrypt(String plaintext, Uint8List key) {
    final iv = _generateSecureRandom(_ivLength);
    final cipher = GCMBlockCipher(AESEngine())
      ..init(
        true,
        AEADParameters(
          KeyParameter(key),
          128, // tag length in bits
          iv,
          Uint8List(0), // associated data
        ),
      );

    final plaintextBytes = Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(plaintext));
    final ciphertext = cipher.process(plaintextBytes);

    // Prepend IV to ciphertext
    final result = Uint8List(iv.length + ciphertext.length);
    result.setAll(0, iv);
    result.setAll(iv.length, ciphertext);

    return base64Encode(result);
  }

  /// Decrypt data using AES-256-GCM
  String? _decrypt(String encryptedData, Uint8List key) {
    try {
      final data = base64Decode(encryptedData);
      if (data.length < _ivLength) return null;

      final iv = data.sublist(0, _ivLength);
      final ciphertext = data.sublist(_ivLength);

      final cipher = GCMBlockCipher(AESEngine())
        ..init(false, AEADParameters(KeyParameter(key), 128, iv, Uint8List(0)));

      final plaintext = cipher.process(ciphertext);
      return utf8.decode(plaintext);
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Compute HMAC for data integrity
  String _computeHmac(String data, Uint8List key) {
    final hmac = HMac(SHA256Digest(), 64)..init(KeyParameter(key));
    final hash = hmac.process(utf8.encode(data));
    return base64Encode(hash);
  }

  /// Verify HMAC
  bool _verifyHmac(String data, String storedHmac, Uint8List key) {
    final computedHmac = _computeHmac(data, key);
    return computedHmac == storedHmac;
  }

  /// Save servers with encryption
  Future<EncryptedResult<void>> saveServers(List<ServerConfig> servers) async {
    if (_prefs == null) {
      final initResult = await initialize();
      if (initResult.isFailure) return initResult;
    }

    try {
      final jsonString = jsonEncode(servers.map((s) => s.toJson()).toList());

      // Encrypt the data
      final encrypted = _encrypt(jsonString, _encryptionKey!);

      // Compute HMAC for integrity
      final hmac = _computeHmac(jsonString, _encryptionKey!);

      // Store encrypted data and HMAC
      await _prefs!.setString(_serversKey, encrypted);
      await _prefs!.setString(_hmacKey, hmac);

      return EncryptedResult.success(null);
    } catch (e) {
      return EncryptedResult.failure('Failed to save servers: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Load and decrypt servers
  Future<EncryptedResult<List<ServerConfig>>> loadServers() async {
    if (_prefs == null) {
      final initResult = await initialize();
      if (initResult.isFailure)
        return EncryptedResult.failure(initResult.error);
    }

    try {
      final encrypted = _prefs!.getString(_serversKey);
      final storedHmac = _prefs!.getString(_hmacKey);

      if (encrypted == null) {
        // No data yet
        return EncryptedResult.success(<ServerConfig>[]);
      }

      // Decrypt
      final decrypted = _decrypt(encrypted, _encryptionKey!);
      if (decrypted == null) {
        return EncryptedResult.failure(
          'Decryption failed - data may be corrupted',
        );
      }

      // Verify HMAC
      if (storedHmac != null &&
          !_verifyHmac(decrypted, storedHmac, _encryptionKey!)) {
        return EncryptedResult.failure(
          'Data integrity check failed - HMAC mismatch',
        );
      }

      // Parse JSON
      final List<dynamic> jsonList = jsonDecode(decrypted);
      final servers = jsonList
          .map((json) => ServerConfig.fromJson(json as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();

      return EncryptedResult.success(servers);
    } catch (e) {
      return EncryptedResult.failure('Failed to load servers: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Migrate from legacy unencrypted storage
  Future<EncryptedResult<void>> migrate() async {
    try {
      _prefs ??= await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final legacyData = _prefs!.getString(_legacyServersKey);

      if (legacyData == null || legacyData.isEmpty) {
        return EncryptedResult.success(null); // Nothing to migrate
      }

      // Parse legacy data
      final List<dynamic> jsonList = jsonDecode(legacyData);
      final servers = jsonList
          .map((json) => ServerConfig.fromJson(json as Map<String, dynamic>))
          .toList();

      // Save with encryption
      final saveResult = await saveServers(servers);
      if (saveResult.isFailure) {
        return saveResult;
      }

      // Remove legacy data
      await _prefs!.remove(_legacyServersKey);

      return EncryptedResult.success(null);
    } catch (e) {
      return EncryptedResult.failure('Migration failed: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Check if migration is needed
  Future<bool> needsMigration() async {
    _prefs ??= await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    return _prefs!.containsKey(_legacyServersKey);
  }

  /// Check if encrypted data exists
  Future<bool> hasEncryptedData() async {
    _prefs ??= await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    return _prefs!.containsKey(_serversKey);
  }

  /// Clear all stored data
  Future<EncryptedResult<void>> clear() async {
    try {
      _prefs ??= await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await _prefs!.remove(_serversKey);
      await _prefs!.remove(_hmacKey);
      await _prefs!.remove(_keyStorageKey);
      _encryptionKey = null;
      return EncryptedResult.success(null);
    } catch (e) {
      return EncryptedResult.failure('Failed to clear data: $e');
    }
  }
}

// lib/services/latency_checker.dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../core/logging/session_logger.dart';
import '../vpn/models/server_models.dart';

/// Result of a latency check operation
class LatencyResult {
  final ServerProfile server;
  final int latencyMs;
  final LatencyCheckMethod method;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final bool success;
  final String? errorMessage;

  const LatencyResult({
    required this.server,
    required this.latencyMs,
    required this.method,
    required this.timestamp,
    required this.success,
    this.errorMessage,
  });

  @override
  String toString() => success
      ? 'LatencyResult(server: ${server.name}, latency: ${latencyMs}ms, method: $method)'
      : 'LatencyResult(server: ${server.name}, failed: $errorMessage)';
}

/// Options for latency checking
class LatencyCheckOptions {
  final Duration timeout;
  final int maxRetries;
  final List<String> testEndpoints;
  final LatencyCheckMethod preferredMethod;
  final bool cacheResults;
  final Duration cacheDuration;

  const LatencyCheckOptions({
    this.timeout = const Duration(seconds: 5),
    this.maxRetries = 2,
    this.testEndpoints = const [],
    this.preferredMethod = LatencyCheckMethod.httpHead,
    this.cacheResults = true,
    this.cacheDuration = const Duration(minutes: 2),
  });

  factory LatencyCheckOptions.defaultOptions() => const LatencyCheckOptions();

  LatencyCheckOptions copyWith({
    Duration? timeout,
    int? maxRetries,
    List<String>? testEndpoints,
    LatencyCheckMethod? preferredMethod,
    bool? cacheResults,
    Duration? cacheDuration,
  }) {
    return LatencyCheckOptions(
      timeout: timeout ?? this.timeout,
      maxRetries: maxRetries ?? this.maxRetries,
      testEndpoints: testEndpoints ?? this.testEndpoints,
      preferredMethod: preferredMethod ?? this.preferredMethod,
      cacheResults: cacheResults ?? this.cacheResults,
      cacheDuration: cacheDuration ?? this.cacheDuration,
    );
  }
}

/// Method used for latency checking
enum LatencyCheckMethod { httpHead, icmpPing, tcpConnect }

/// Abstract interface for latency checking
abstract class LatencyChecker {
  Future<LatencyResult> checkLatency(
    ServerProfile server, {
    LatencyCheckOptions? options,
  });

  Future<List<LatencyResult>> checkMultipleServers(
    List<ServerProfile> servers, {
    int maxConcurrent = 5,
  });

  void clearCache();
}

/// Default implementation of LatencyChecker with multi-strategy support
class DefaultLatencyChecker implements LatencyChecker {
  DefaultLatencyChecker({required SessionLogger logger})
    : _logger = logger,
      _httpClient = http.Client();

  final http.Client _httpClient;
  final SessionLogger _logger;
  final Map<String, _CachedLatency> _cache = {};

  static const List<String> _defaultEndpoints = [
    'http://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_200',
    'http://1.1.1.1/cdn-cgi/trace',
    'http://clients3.google.com/generate_204',
  ];

  @override
  Future<LatencyResult> checkLatency(
    ServerProfile server, {
    LatencyCheckOptions? options,
  }) async {
    final opts = options ?? LatencyCheckOptions.defaultOptions();
    final cacheKey = '${server.id}_${opts.preferredMethod.name}';

    // Check cache first
    if (opts.cacheResults && _cache.containsKey(cacheKey)) {
      final cached = _cache[cacheKey]!;
      if (DateTime.now().difference(cached.timestamp) < opts.cacheDuration) {
        _logger.debug(
          'Latency cache hit for ${server.name}: ${cached.result.latencyMs}ms',
        );
        return cached.result;
      }
    }

    // Try each attempt
    for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= opts.maxRetries; attempt++) {
      try {
        final result = await _measureLatency(server, opts);
        if (result.success) {
          if (opts.cacheResults) {
            _cache[cacheKey] = _CachedLatency(result, DateTime.now());
          }
          _logger.info(
            'Latency check for ${server.name}: ${result.latencyMs}ms via ${result.method.name}',
          );
          return result;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        _logger.debug(
          'Latency check attempt ${attempt + 1} failed for ${server.name}: $e',
        );
      }
    }

    return LatencyResult(
      server: server,
      latencyMs: 9999,
      method: opts.preferredMethod,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      success: false,
      errorMessage: 'All retry attempts failed',
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<List<LatencyResult>> checkMultipleServers(
    List<ServerProfile> servers, {
    int maxConcurrent = 5,
  }) async {
    if (servers.isEmpty) return [];

    final results = <LatencyResult>[];
    final semaphore = _Semaphore(maxConcurrent);

    final futures = servers.map((server) async {
      await semaphore.acquire();
      try {
        final result = await checkLatency(server);
        results.add(result);
      } finally {
        semaphore.release();
      }
    });

    await Future.wait(futures);

    // Sort by latency ascending
    return results..sort((a, b) => a.latencyMs.compareTo(b.latencyMs));
  }

  @override
  void clearCache() {
    _cache.clear();
    _logger.debug('Latency cache cleared');
  }

  Future<LatencyResult> _measureLatency(
    ServerProfile server,
    LatencyCheckOptions opts,
  ) async {
    switch (opts.preferredMethod) {
      case LatencyCheckMethod.httpHead:
        return _httpHeadCheck(server, opts);
      case LatencyCheckMethod.icmpPing:
        return _icmpCheck(server, opts);
      case LatencyCheckMethod.tcpConnect:
        return _tcpCheck(server, opts);
    }
  }

  Future<LatencyResult> _httpHeadCheck(
    ServerProfile server,
    LatencyCheckOptions opts,
  ) async {
    final endpoints = opts.testEndpoints.isNotEmpty
        ? opts.testEndpoints
        : _defaultEndpoints;

    for (final endpoint in endpoints) {
      final stopwatch = Stopwatch()..start();
      try {
        final response = await _httpClient
            .head(Uri.parse(endpoint))
            .timeout(opts.timeout);
        stopwatch.stop();
        if (response.statusCode == 200) {
          return LatencyResult(
            server: server,
            latencyMs: stopwatch.elapsedMilliseconds,
            method: LatencyCheckMethod.httpHead,
            timestamp: DateTime.now(),
            success: true,
          );
        }
      } catch (_) {
        // Try next endpoint
        continue;
      }
    }

    return LatencyResult(
      server: server,
      latencyMs: 9999,
      method: LatencyCheckMethod.httpHead,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(),
      success: false,
      errorMessage: 'All HTTP endpoints failed',
    );
  }

  Future<LatencyResult> _icmpCheck(
    ServerProfile server,
    LatencyCheckOptions opts,
  ) async {
    // ICMP ping requires platform-specific implementation
    // On most platforms, this requires elevated privileges
    // For cross-platform support, we fall back to TCP connect
    try {
      final result = await _invokePlatformIcmp(
        server.host,
        timeout: opts.timeout,
      );
      return LatencyResult(
        server: server,
        latencyMs: result.round(),
        method: LatencyCheckMethod.icmpPing,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        success: true,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return LatencyResult(
        server: server,
        latencyMs: 9999,
        method: LatencyCheckMethod.icmpPing,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        success: false,
        errorMessage: 'ICMP not available: $e',
      );
    }
  }

  Future<LatencyResult> _tcpCheck(
    ServerProfile server,
    LatencyCheckOptions opts,
  ) async {
    final stopwatch = Stopwatch()..start();
    try {
      final socket = await Socket.connect(
        server.host,
        server.port,
        timeout: opts.timeout,
      );
      stopwatch.stop();
      socket.destroy();
      return LatencyResult(
        server: server,
        latencyMs: stopwatch.elapsedMilliseconds,
        method: LatencyCheckMethod.tcpConnect,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        success: true,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      stopwatch.stop();
      return LatencyResult(
        server: server,
        latencyMs: 9999,
        method: LatencyCheckMethod.tcpConnect,
        timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        success: false,
        errorMessage: 'TCP connect failed: $e',
      );
    }
  }

  Future<double> _invokePlatformIcmp(
    String host, {
    required Duration timeout,
  }) async {
    // Platform channel integration point for native ICMP
    // This would be implemented via platform channels on each platform
    // For now, throw to indicate not available
    throw UnimplementedError(
      'ICMP ping requires platform channel implementation',
    );
  }
}

/// Cache entry for latency results
class _CachedLatency {
  final LatencyResult result;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  _CachedLatency(this.result, this.timestamp);
}

/// Semaphore for limiting concurrent operations
class _Semaphore {
  int _permits;
  final List<void Function()> _waitQueue = [];

  _Semaphore(this._permits);

  Future<void> acquire() async {
    if (_permits > 0) {
      _permits--;
      return;
    }
    final completer = Completer<void>();
    _waitQueue.add(completer.complete);
    await completer.future;
  }

  void release() {
    if (_waitQueue.isNotEmpty) {
      final resolve = _waitQueue.removeAt(0);
      resolve();
    } else {
      _permits++;
    }
  }
}

/// Factory for creating latency checker based on platform
class LatencyCheckerFactory {
  static LatencyChecker create({required SessionLogger logger}) {
    return DefaultLatencyChecker(logger: logger);
  }

  static LatencyCheckMethod suggestMethodForPlatform() {
    if (Platform.isWindows) {
      // Desktop platforms can use ICMP if available
      return LatencyCheckMethod.tcpConnect;
    }
    return LatencyCheckMethod.httpHead;
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

class MarzbanApi {
  final String _baseUrl;
  final String _username;
  final String _password;
  String? _token;
  final http.Client _client = http.Client();

  MarzbanApi(this._baseUrl, this._username, this._password);

  Future<String> _getToken() async {
    if (_token != null) return _token!;

    final uri = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/api/admin/token');
    final response = await _client.post(
      uri,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},
      body: {'username': _username, 'password': _password},
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('Auth failed: ${response.statusCode} ${response.body}');
    }

    final data = jsonDecode(response.body);
    _token = data['access_token'] as String?;
    if (_token == null) throw Exception('No token in response');
    return _token!;
  }

  Future<String?> getSubscriptionUrl(String username) async {
    final token = await _getToken();
    final response = await _client.get(
      Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/api/user/$username/links'),
      headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer $token'},
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) return null;
    final data = jsonDecode(response.body);
    return data['vless']?.first ??
        data['vmess']?.first ??
        data['trojan']?.first;
  }

  Future<List<UserInfo>?> getUsers() async {
    final token = await _getToken();
    final response = await _client.get(
      Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/api/users'),
      headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer $token'},
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) return null;
    final list = jsonDecode(response.body) as List<dynamic>;
    return list.map((e) => UserInfo.fromJson(e)).toList();
  }

  Future<bool> createUser(
    String username, {
    int dataLimitGB = 100,
    int expireDays = 30,
  }) async {
    final token = await _getToken();
    final expireAt =
        DateTime.now()
            .toUtc()
            .add(Duration(days: expireDays))
            .millisecondsSinceEpoch ~/
        1000;

    final body = jsonEncode({
      'username': username,
      'status': 'active',
      'data_limit': dataLimitGB * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
      'expire': expireAt,
    });

    final response = await _client.post(
      Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/api/user'),
      headers: {
        'Authorization': 'Bearer $token',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: body,
    );

    return response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201;
  }

  Future<String?> createVpnKey({int durationDays = 30}) async {
    try {
      final token = await _getToken();
      final expireAt =
          DateTime.now()
              .toUtc()
              .add(Duration(days: durationDays))
              .millisecondsSinceEpoch ~/
          1000;
      final randomId = _randomId(12);
      final username = 'client_$randomId';

      final body = jsonEncode({
        'username': username,
        'proxies': {
          'vless': {'flow': 'xtls-rprx-vision'},
        },
        'inbounds': {
          'vless': ['VLESS Reality'],
        },
        'expire': expireAt,
        'data_limit': 0,
        'note': 'SpeedVPS:$randomId',
      });

      final response = await _client.post(
        Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/api/user'),
        headers: {
          'Authorization': 'Bearer $token',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: body,
      );

      if (response.statusCode != 200 && response.statusCode != 201) return null;

      final userData = jsonDecode(response.body);
      String? subUrl = userData['subscription_url'];
      if (subUrl != null && !subUrl.startsWith('http')) {
        subUrl = '$_baseUrl$subUrl';
      }

      if (subUrl != null) {
        final subResponse = await _client.get(Uri.parse(subUrl));
        if (subResponse.statusCode == 200) {
          final lines = const LineSplitter().convert(subResponse.body);
          for (final line in lines) {
            if (line.trim().startsWith('vless://')) return line.trim();
          }
        }
      }

      final links = userData['links'] as List<dynamic>?;
      if (links != null && links.isNotEmpty) return links.first as String;

      return null;
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  String _randomId(int length) {
    const chars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789';
    final sb = StringBuffer();
    for (var i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      sb.write(chars[(DateTime.now().microsecond + i) % chars.length]);
    }
    return sb.toString();
  }

  void dispose() => _client.close();
}

class UserInfo {
  final String username;
  final String status;
  final int dataLimit;
  final int expire;

  UserInfo({
    required this.username,
    required this.status,
    required this.dataLimit,
    required this.expire,
  });

  factory UserInfo.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => UserInfo(
    username: json['username'] ?? '',
    status: json['status'] ?? '',
    dataLimit: json['data_limit'] ?? 0,
    expire: json['expire'] ?? 0,
  );
}

import '../core/logging/session_logger.dart';
import '../vpn/models/server_models.dart';
import 'latency_checker.dart';

class ServerRepository {
  final List<ServerProfile> _servers = <ServerProfile>[];
  String? _selectedServerId;
  final LatencyChecker _latencyChecker;

  ServerRepository({
    required SessionLogger logger,
    LatencyChecker? latencyChecker,
  }) : _latencyChecker =
           latencyChecker ?? LatencyCheckerFactory.create(logger: logger);

  List<ServerProfile> get servers => List<ServerProfile>.unmodifiable(_servers);

  String? get selectedServerId => _selectedServerId;

  ServerProfile? get selectedServer {
    if (_selectedServerId == null) {
      return null;
    }
    for (final ServerProfile server in _servers) {
      if (server.id == _selectedServerId) {
        return server;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  void upsert(ServerProfile profile) {
    final int index = _servers.indexWhere(
      (ServerProfile item) => item.id == profile.id,
    );
    if (index >= 0) {
      _servers[index] = profile;
      return;
    }
    _servers.add(profile);
  }

  bool remove(String id) {
    final int initialLength = _servers.length;
    _servers.removeWhere((ServerProfile item) => item.id == id);
    final bool removed = _servers.length != initialLength;
    if (removed && _selectedServerId == id) {
      _selectedServerId = null;
    }
    return removed;
  }

  bool select(String id) {
    final bool exists = _servers.any((ServerProfile server) => server.id == id);
    if (exists) {
      _selectedServerId = id;
    }
    return exists;
  }

  Future<ServerProfile> updateLatency(
    ServerProfile profile, {
    LatencyCheckOptions? options,
  }) async {
    final result = await _latencyChecker.checkLatency(
      profile,
      options: options,
    );
    final ServerProfile updated = profile.copyWith(
      latencyMs: result.success ? result.latencyMs : 9999,
    );
    upsert(updated);
    return updated;
  }

  Future<void> refreshAllLatencies({
    LatencyCheckOptions? options,
    int maxConcurrent = 5,
  }) async {
    final results = await _latencyChecker.checkMultipleServers(
      List<ServerProfile>.from(_servers),
      maxConcurrent: maxConcurrent,
    );

    for (final result in results) {
      final index = _servers.indexWhere((s) => s.id == result.server.id);
      if (index >= 0) {
        _servers[index] = _servers[index].copyWith(
          latencyMs: result.success ? result.latencyMs : 9999,
        );
      }
    }

    _servers.sort((ServerProfile a, ServerProfile b) {
      final int la = a.latencyMs ?? 9999;
      final int lb = b.latencyMs ?? 9999;
      return la.compareTo(lb);
    });
  }

  void clearLatencyCache() {
    _latencyChecker.clearCache();
  }
}

// filepath: lib/services/subscription_service.dart

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../models/server_config.dart';

class SubscriptionService {
  /// Fetches a subscription URL and parses VLESS links from the base64 response.
  Future<List<ServerConfig>> fetchSubscription(String url) async {
    final response = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(url),
      headers: {'User-Agent': 'SpeedVPS/1.0 (sing-box)'},
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('Subscription fetch failed: HTTP ${response.statusCode}');
    }

    final body = response.body.trim();
    String decoded;
    try {
      // Marzban returns base64-encoded list of URIs separated by newlines
      decoded = utf8.decode(base64Decode(_padBase64(body)));
    } catch (_) {
      // Maybe it's already plain text
      decoded = body;
    }

    final lines = decoded
        .split('\n')
        .map((l) => l.trim())
        .where((l) => l.isNotEmpty);
    final servers = <ServerConfig>[];

    for (final line in lines) {
      if (line.startsWith('vless://')) {
        try {
          servers.add(ServerConfig.fromVlessUri(line));
        } catch (e) {
          // Skip unparseable lines, but log
          print('Failed to parse VLESS URI: $line, error: $e');
        }
      }
    }

    return servers;
  }

  String _padBase64(String input) {
    final remainder = input.length % 4;
    if (remainder == 0) return input;
    return input + '=' * (4 - remainder);
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../ui/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Сервис выбора стилизации (4 варианта из дизайна SpeedVPS).
/// Применяет палитру к [AppColors] и уведомляет слушателей.
class ThemeService extends ChangeNotifier {
  static const String _variantKey = 'speedvps_theme_variant';

  final SharedPreferences _prefs;

  ThemeService(this._prefs) {
    AppColors.setVariant(variant);
  }

  AppThemeVariant get variant {
    final index = _prefs.getInt(_variantKey) ?? 0;
    return AppThemeVariant.values[index.clamp(
      0,
      AppThemeVariant.values.length - 1,
    )];
  }

  Future<void> setVariant(AppThemeVariant value) async {
    if (value == variant) return;
    await _prefs.setInt(_variantKey, value.index);
    AppColors.setVariant(value);
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_cache_manager/flutter_cache_manager.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class WindowsAppInfo {
  final String appId;
  final String displayName;
  final String executablePath;
  final String processName;

  WindowsAppInfo({
    required this.appId,
    required this.displayName,
    required this.executablePath,
    required this.processName,
  });

  String get iconCacheKey => 'app_icon_${appId.hashCode}';
}

class WindowsAppsService {
  static const String _selectedAppsKey = 'selected_apps';

  /// Надёжный скрипт PowerShell для получения списка приложений.
  Future<List<WindowsAppInfo>> getInstalledApps() async {
    final script = r'''
$apps = @()

# 1. Пытаемся получить Win32 приложения из реестра
$uninstallPaths = @(
    "HKLM:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*",
    "HKLM:\Software\WOW6432Node\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*",
    "HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\*"
)

foreach ($path in $uninstallPaths) {
    Get-ItemProperty -Path $path -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object {
        if ($_.DisplayName -and $_.DisplayName -notmatch '^\{|Update|Driver|Package|Microsoft (Visual|\.NET|ASP)|Windows (SDK|Driver)') {
            $exe = $_.DisplayIcon
            if (-not $exe) { $exe = $_.InstallLocation }
            if ($exe -and ($exe -match '\.exe')) {
                $exePath = $exe -replace ',[0-9]+$',''
                if (Test-Path $exePath) {
                    $apps += [PSCustomObject]@{
                        AppId = $exePath
                        DisplayName = $_.DisplayName
                        ExecutablePath = $exePath
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

# 2. Если реестр не дал результатов, добавляем системные приложения вручную
if ($apps.Count -eq 0) {
    $systemApps = @(
        @{ Name='Notepad'; Path='C:\Windows\System32\notepad.exe' },
        @{ Name='Calculator'; Path='C:\Windows\System32\calc.exe' },
        @{ Name='Command Prompt'; Path='C:\Windows\System32\cmd.exe' },
        @{ Name='PowerShell'; Path='C:\Windows\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe' },
        @{ Name='Task Manager'; Path='C:\Windows\System32\Taskmgr.exe' }
    )
    foreach ($app in $systemApps) {
        if (Test-Path $app.Path) {
            $apps += [PSCustomObject]@{
                AppId = $app.Path
                DisplayName = $app.Name
                ExecutablePath = $app.Path
            }
        }
    }
}

# 3. Удаляем дубликаты и выводим JSON
$apps | Sort-Object DisplayName | Select-Object -Unique | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
''';

    try {
      final result = await Process.run('powershell', [
        '-NoProfile',
        '-ExecutionPolicy',
        'Bypass',
        '-Command',
        script,
      ], runInShell: true);

      if (result.exitCode != 0) {
        throw Exception('PowerShell error: ${result.stderr}');
      }

      final output = result.stdout.toString().trim();
      if (output.isEmpty) return [];

      final List<dynamic> jsonList = jsonDecode(output);
      final apps = <WindowsAppInfo>[];

      for (var item in jsonList) {
        final exePath = item['ExecutablePath'] as String? ?? '';
        if (exePath.isEmpty) continue;

        final processName = exePath.split('\\').last;
        apps.add(
          WindowsAppInfo(
            appId: item['AppId'] as String,
            displayName: item['DisplayName'] as String,
            executablePath: exePath,
            processName: processName,
          ),
        );
      }

      return apps;
    } catch (e) {
      // Если совсем ничего не получилось, возвращаем пустой список
      print('Error getting installed apps: $e');
      return [];
    }
  }

  /// Временно отключаем извлечение иконок
  Future<String?> getAppIconBase64(String executablePath) async {
    return null;
  }

  Future<File?> getCachedIconFile(WindowsAppInfo app) async {
    try {
      final cacheKey = app.iconCacheKey;
      final fileInfo = await DefaultCacheManager().getFileFromCache(cacheKey);
      if (fileInfo != null) return fileInfo.file;

      final base64 = await getAppIconBase64(app.executablePath);
      if (base64 == null) return null;

      final bytes = base64Decode(base64);
      return await DefaultCacheManager().putFile(
        cacheKey,
        bytes,
        fileExtension: 'png',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveSelectedApps(Set<String> selectedAppIds) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setStringList(_selectedAppsKey, selectedAppIds.toList());
  }

  Future<Set<String>> loadSelectedApps() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final list = prefs.getStringList(_selectedAppsKey) ?? [];
    return list.toSet();
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

/// API client for 3X-UI panel (v3.x)
/// Works with Bearer token authentication.
class X3UIApi {
  final String _baseUrl;
  final String _token;
  final http.Client _client = http.Client();

  X3UIApi(this._baseUrl, this._token);

  // ── helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  Map<String, String> get _headers => {
        'Authorization': 'Bearer $_token',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Accept': 'application/json',
      };

  Future<T> _post<T>(String path, Object? body, T Function(Map<String, dynamic>) parser) async {
    final uri = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl$path');
    final response = await _client.post(
      uri,
      headers: _headers,
      body: body == null ? null : jsonEncode(body),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('POST $path failed: ${response.statusCode} ${response.body}');
    }

    final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    if (data['success'] != true) {
      throw Exception('API error: ${data['msg']}');
    }
    return parser(data);
  }

  Future<T> _get<T>(String path, T Function(Map<String, dynamic>) parser) async {
    final uri = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl$path');
    final response = await _client.get(uri, headers: _headers);

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('GET $path failed: ${response.statusCode} ${response.body}');
    }

    final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    if (data['success'] != true) {
      throw Exception('API error: ${data['msg']}');
    }
    return parser(data);
  }

  // ── inbounds ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// List all inbounds with their client stats.
  Future<List<InboundInfo>> listInbounds() async {
    return _get('/panel/api/inbounds/list', (data) {
      final list = data['obj'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
      return list.map((e) => InboundInfo.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>)).toList();
    });
  }

  /// Lightweight inbound list for pickers (id, remark, protocol, port).
  Future<List<InboundOption>> listInboundOptions() async {
    return _get('/panel/api/inbounds/options', (data) {
      final list = data['obj'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
      return list.map((e) => InboundOption.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>)).toList();
    });
  }

  // ── clients ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// List every client with attached inbound IDs and traffic record.
  Future<List<ClientInfo>> listClients() async {
    return _get('/panel/api/clients/list', (data) {
      final list = data['obj'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
      return list.map((e) => ClientInfo.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>)).toList();
    });
  }

  /// Get one client by email.
  Future<ClientInfo?> getClient(String email) async {
    try {
      return _get('/panel/api/clients/get/${Uri.encodeComponent(email)}', (data) {
        final client = data['obj']?['client'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
        if (client == null) throw Exception('No client data');
        return ClientInfo.fromJson(client);
      });
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Create a new client attached to given inbounds.
  /// [totalGB] — traffic limit in GB (0 = unlimited)
  /// [expiryTime] — Unix timestamp in milliseconds
  Future<bool> addClient({
    required String email,
    required List<int> inboundIds,
    int totalGB = 0,
    required int expiryTimeMs,
    String? subId,
    bool enable = true,
    String? comment,
  }) async {
    try {
      final body = {
        'client': {
          'email': email,
          if (subId != null) 'subId': subId,
          'totalGB': totalGB == 0 ? 0 : totalGB * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
          'expiryTime': expiryTimeMs,
          'enable': enable,
          if (comment != null) 'comment': comment,
        },
        'inboundIds': inboundIds,
      };

      final result = await _post('/panel/api/clients/add', body, (data) => data);
      return result['success'] == true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Update an existing client (full replace).
  Future<bool> updateClient({
    required String email,
    int? totalGB,
    int? expiryTimeMs,
    bool? enable,
  }) async {
    try {
      final body = <String, dynamic>{
        'email': email,
        if (totalGB != null) 'totalGB': totalGB == 0 ? 0 : totalGB * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
        if (expiryTimeMs != null) 'expiryTime': expiryTimeMs,
        if (enable != null) 'enable': enable,
      };

      final result = await _post(
        '/panel/api/clients/update/${Uri.encodeComponent(email)}',
        body,
        (data) => data,
      );
      return result['success'] == true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Delete a client by email.
  Future<bool> deleteClient(String email) async {
    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse('$_baseUrl/panel/api/clients/del/${Uri.encodeComponent(email)}');
      final response = await _client.post(uri, headers: _headers);
      if (response.statusCode != 200) return false;
      final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return data['success'] == true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ── subscription link ────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Build subscription URL for a client by subId.
  /// 3X-UI serves subscriptions at a separate port/path.
  String getSubscriptionUrl(String subId) {
    // Subscription server runs on port 2096 with path /aL9xK3mP/
    return 'https://109.120.176.131:2096/aL9xK3mP/${Uri.encodeComponent(subId)}';
  }

  /// Get raw subscription content (usually base64 or plain text with links).
  Future<String?> fetchSubscriptionContent(String subId) async {
    try {
      final url = getSubscriptionUrl(subId);
      final response = await _client.get(Uri.parse(url), headers: _headers);
      if (response.statusCode != 200) return null;
      return response.body;
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // ── utility ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /// Generate a fresh UUID v4 from the server.
  Future<String?> getNewUuid() async {
    try {
      return _get('/panel/api/server/getNewUUID', (data) => data['obj'] as String?);
    } catch (e) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  void dispose() => _client.close();
}

class InboundInfo {
  final int id;
  final String remark;
  final String protocol;
  final int port;
  final bool enable;

  InboundInfo({
    required this.id,
    required this.remark,
    required this.protocol,
    required this.port,
    required this.enable,
  });

  factory InboundInfo.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => InboundInfo(
        id: json['id'] ?? 0,
        remark: json['remark'] ?? '',
        protocol: json['protocol'] ?? '',
        port: json['port'] ?? 0,
        enable: json['enable'] ?? false,
      );
}

class InboundOption {
  final int id;
  final String remark;
  final String protocol;
  final int port;
  final bool tlsFlowCapable;

  InboundOption({
    required this.id,
    required this.remark,
    required this.protocol,
    required this.port,
    required this.tlsFlowCapable,
  });

  factory InboundOption.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => InboundOption(
        id: json['id'] ?? 0,
        remark: json['remark'] ?? '',
        protocol: json['protocol'] ?? '',
        port: json['port'] ?? 0,
        tlsFlowCapable: json['tlsFlowCapable'] ?? false,
      );
}

class ClientInfo {
  final int id;
  final String email;
  final String? subId;
  final String? uuid;
  final int totalGB;
  final int expiryTime;
  final bool enable;
  final List<int> inboundIds;
  final ClientTraffic? traffic;

  ClientInfo({
    required this.id,
    required this.email,
    this.subId,
    this.uuid,
    required this.totalGB,
    required this.expiryTime,
    required this.enable,
    required this.inboundIds,
    this.traffic,
  });

  factory ClientInfo.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ClientInfo(
        id: json['id'] ?? 0,
        email: json['email'] ?? '',
        subId: json['subId'],
        uuid: json['uuid'],
        totalGB: json['totalGB'] ?? 0,
        expiryTime: json['expiryTime'] ?? 0,
        enable: json['enable'] ?? false,
        inboundIds: (json['inboundIds'] as List<dynamic>? ?? []).cast<int>(),
        traffic: json['traffic'] == null
            ? null
            : ClientTraffic.fromJson(json['traffic'] as Map<String, dynamic>),
      );

  /// Expiry as DateTime.
  DateTime? get expiryDate =>
      expiryTime > 0 ? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(expiryTime) : null;

  /// True if expired.
  bool get isExpired =>
      expiryTime > 0 && DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch > expiryTime;
}

class ClientTraffic {
  final int up;
  final int down;
  final bool enable;

  ClientTraffic({required this.up, required this.down, required this.enable});

  factory ClientTraffic.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ClientTraffic(
        up: json['up'] ?? 0,
        down: json['down'] ?? 0,
        enable: json['enable'] ?? false,
      );
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import '../models/subscription_models.dart';

class YooKassaService {
  final String _shopId;
  final String _secretKey;
  static const String _apiBaseUrl = 'https://api.yookassa.ru/v3';
  final http.Client _client = http.Client();

  YooKassaService(this._shopId, this._secretKey);

  Future<YooKassaPaymentResult?> createPayment(
    double amountRub,
    String description,
    String returnUrl, {
    Map<String, String>? metadata,
  }) async {
    try {
      final body = jsonEncode({
        'amount': {'value': amountRub.toStringAsFixed(2), 'currency': 'RUB'},
        'capture': true,
        'confirmation': {'type': 'redirect', 'return_url': returnUrl},
        'description': description,
        'metadata': metadata ?? {},
      });

      final credentials = base64Encode(utf8.encode('$_shopId:$_secretKey'));
      final request = http.Request('POST', Uri.parse('$_apiBaseUrl/payments'))
        ..headers['Authorization'] = 'Basic $credentials'
        ..headers['Idempotence-Key'] = _generateIdempotenceKey()
        ..headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'
        ..body = body;

      final streamed = await _client
          .send(request)
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 30));
      final response = await http.Response.fromStream(streamed);

      if (response.statusCode < 200 || response.statusCode >= 300) {
        await _logError('CreatePayment', response.statusCode, response.body);
        return null;
      }

      final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return YooKassaPaymentResult(
        paymentId: data['id'] ?? '',
        confirmationUrl: data['confirmation']?['confirmation_url'] ?? '',
        status: _parseStatus(data['status']),
        amount: amountRub,
        description: description,
        metadata: metadata ?? {},
      );
    } catch (e, st) {
      await _logError('CreatePayment', 0, 'Exception: $e\n$st');
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<PaymentStatus> getPaymentStatus(String paymentId) async {
    try {
      final credentials = base64Encode(utf8.encode('$_shopId:$_secretKey'));
      final response = await _client
          .get(
            Uri.parse('$_apiBaseUrl/payments/$paymentId'),
            headers: {'Authorization': 'Basic $credentials'},
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 30));

      if (response.statusCode != 200) return PaymentStatus.failed;
      final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return _parseStatus(data['status']);
    } catch (e) {
      return PaymentStatus.failed;
    }
  }

  /// Автоматическое подтверждение платежа (capture) если он в статусе waiting_for_capture
  Future<PaymentStatus> capturePayment(String paymentId, double amountRub) async {
    try {
      final body = jsonEncode({
        'amount': {'value': amountRub.toStringAsFixed(2), 'currency': 'RUB'},
      });

      final credentials = base64Encode(utf8.encode('$_shopId:$_secretKey'));
      final response = await _client
          .post(
            Uri.parse('$_apiBaseUrl/payments/$paymentId/capture'),
            headers: {
              'Authorization': 'Basic $credentials',
              'Idempotence-Key': _generateIdempotenceKey(),
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: body,
          )
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 30));

      if (response.statusCode != 200) return PaymentStatus.failed;
      final data = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return _parseStatus(data['status']);
    } catch (e) {
      return PaymentStatus.failed;
    }
  }

  /// Polling проверки статуса платежа с заданным интервалом
  Stream<PaymentStatus> pollPaymentStatus(
    String paymentId, {
    Duration interval = const Duration(seconds: 10),
    Duration timeout = const Duration(minutes: 10),
  }) async* {
    final stopwatch = Stopwatch()..start();
    while (stopwatch.elapsed < timeout) {
      final status = await getPaymentStatus(paymentId);
      yield status;
      if (status == PaymentStatus.succeeded ||
          status == PaymentStatus.canceled ||
          status == PaymentStatus.failed) {
        break;
      }
      await Future.delayed(interval);
    }
    stopwatch.stop();
  }

  PaymentStatus _parseStatus(String? status) {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'pending':
      case 'waiting_for_capture':
        return PaymentStatus.pending;
      case 'succeeded':
        return PaymentStatus.succeeded;
      case 'canceled':
        return PaymentStatus.canceled;
      case 'failed':
        return PaymentStatus.failed;
      default:
        return PaymentStatus.failed;
    }
  }

  String _generateIdempotenceKey() {
    final rnd = Random.secure();
    return '${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}_${rnd.nextInt(999999)}';
  }

  Future<void> _logError(String method, int status, String response) async {
    try {
      final dir = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
      final file = File('${dir.path}/SpeedVPS/subscription.log');
      await file.parent.create(recursive: true);
      await file.writeAsString(
        '[${DateTime.now()}] [YooKassa] $method failed: $status - $response\n',
        mode: FileMode.append,
      );
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  void dispose() => _client.close();
}
